FUNDED TRADES (25; only lots the shared allocator funded — refused shadow signals are in signals.csv; $100,000.00 per trade, never compounded)
  fills: the engine's raw execution prices; friction = 5 bps per side on each fill (commission $0); interest = 11% simple on $100,000.00 for the session-date days held (interestDays in the CSV);
  gross P&L = shares × (exit fill − entry fill) before costs; net = gross − friction − interest (the engine's net);
  MAE = the engine's worst minute mark while held (shares × last price − principal − accrued interest).

▶ P1 scenarios
  ✔ a sale due after the last event executes; the replacement is still reserved at the end (0.808485ms)
  ✔ a maximum-hold exit supersedes the switch; the replacement fills minutes later (0.448043ms)
  ✔ a replacement can be preempted from the bar after its purchase (a two-minute chain) (0.528284ms)
  ✔ portfolio mode: margin that binds only with the incumbent open means no preemption (2.293936ms)
  ✔ a closing-print sale executes when the replacement fills at the next session’s open (0.610464ms)
  ✔ an own exit in the decision minute frees the pool: a plain grant, not a preemption (0.536103ms)
  ✔ a friction-driven abort at the same-session next trade (0.471624ms)
  ✔ the $250 buffer: exactly $250 switches, $249.99 does not (0.990186ms)
  ✔ windowed figures equal the engine’s daily marks after the boundary (22.981405ms)
  ✔ a refused signal is never reconsidered: the book is funded only on a later fresh signal (0.742545ms)
  ✔ P0 equals the frozen engine with a fifth, candidate book (3.398523ms)
  ✔ auction prints at the close and at a later session’s opening trade (confirmatory review cases) (1.712091ms)

▶ stop policies
  ✔ net: inactive while a fill at the stop would lose after interest, active once above it, exits at the stop level (1.973694ms)
  ✔ gross breakeven (B-B) latches when the stop level reaches the entry price and holds through a loss-making sale (0.476453ms)
  ✔ gross threshold (B-A) activates at the configured appreciation, not before, and is a latch (0.149211ms)
  ✔ always (B-D) is active from the session after the entry, and a gap through it fills at the open (0.246592ms)
  ✔ two-stage (B-C): the protective stop acts in the EARLY stage, the trailing stop after the breakeven latch (0.292792ms)
  ✔ netLatched never switches off; net can (3.307112ms)
  ✔ replayPosition reproduces the engine exit of a given fill (0.193621ms)
  ✔ the maximum hold still sells a losing position whatever the policy, and the buffer only acts after it applies (0.720534ms)
✔ stop policies (8.014694ms)
▶ entry modes
  ✔ always: arms on the first eligible minute, buys on the rebound from the lowest later low, re-arms after an exit, never on the exit session (0.507403ms)
  ✔ swing: the rolling low excludes the current session, a close at or below it arms, the rebound buys (0.453123ms)
  ✔ C3 arbitration: an armed swing setup yields to the deep path on the minute D1 would arm, and the window stays open (0.354743ms)
  ✔ path causality: nothing arms before the eligibility session, and the rolling low never sees the current session (0.231862ms)
✔ entry modes (1.691562ms)

▶ entry-day activation in a book
  ✔ a same-session adverse move after the fill exits at L that session (next-session waits for the next open) (0.487263ms)
  ✔ a fill-minute low through L exits in the fill minute itself (stop fill) or at the next minute’s open (next-open) (0.275802ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a minute opening through L on the entry day loses more than the 8% distance (0.156511ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a position the entry day did not stop gaps far through L the next morning (0.156851ms)
  ✔ a closing-print fill has nothing to observe: identical to next-session (0.247641ms)
  ✔ an order carried overnight fills at the opening trade and is protected from that trade on (0.201611ms)

  ✔ applies the stop-slippage stress to an entry-day fill (0.127751ms)

▶ the decline gate

  ✔ a profitable exit ends an unblocked episode, and the continuation re-entry is untouched (0.239651ms)
  ✔ never blocks a book without failures (the recovery path is unchanged) (0.181371ms)
✔ the decline gate (1.604861ms)
